	["XP"] = function(p1463, p1464, p1465, p1466, p1467, p1468, p1469, p1470, p1471, p1472) -- name: XP
		if p1470 <= 29 then
			local v1473 = p1472 + (p1465 - 128) * 128
			return 32, 2 + p1468, v1473, p1472, p1464, p1466, p1471, p1469
		end
		if p1470 > 30 then
			local v1474 = p1463[103](p1467, p1468)
			return v1474 < 128 and 39 or 16, p1468, 7, v1474, p1464, p1466, p1471, p1469
		end
		local v1475 = p1465 % 256
		local v1476 = (p1465 - v1475) / 256
		local v1477 = v1476 % 256
		local v1478 = (v1476 - v1477) / 256
		local v1479 = v1478 % 256
		return 18, p1468, v1477, p1472, v1479, (v1478 - v1479) / 256, (v1475 - 37) * 1, 37
	end,
	[101] = coroutine.running,
	["b"] = function(p1480, p1481, p1482, p1483, p1484, p1485, p1486, p1487, p1488, p1489) -- name: b
		if p1483 <= 153 then
			if p1483 <= 152 then
				return 101, p1485, p1489 + 128 * (p1481 - 128), p1487 + 2, p1488, p1482, p1484
			end
			local v1490 = p1480[103](p1489, p1487)
			return v1490 >= 128 and 170 or 62, p1485, p1489, p1487, p1488, p1482, v1490
		end
		if p1483 <= 154 then
			return 71, p1485, p1489, p1487, p1488, p1480[103](p1489, 2 + p1487), p1484
		end
		if p1483 > 155 then
			return 125, p1485[3], p1489, p1487, p1488, p1482, p1484
		end
		local v1491 = p1480[103](p1489, p1487 + 3)
		local v1492 = (p1488 - 128) * 2097152
		local v1493 = p1486 - 128
		local v1494 = 16384 * (p1481 - 128)
